# Sean Parker (p0013)

## Review approach

Use the Parker Foundation's own biography identifying Sean Parker as its president and PICI founder. SEAN N PARKER FOUNDATION and SEAN PARKER FOUNDATION are treated as filing-name variants in this name/leadership context, not global company aliases. Same-name engineers, architects, a produce broker and educator are rejected for substantive occupational conflicts. SHAWN/SHAUN are retrieval hypotheses only and are not accepted variants. APERCEN PARTNERS, PARKER MEDIA and abbreviated/misspelled foundation names need further evidence. Blank and self-employed groups need a direct same-name/location/date anchor.

Examined 130 candidate evidence groups containing 772 source records. Accepted 213 record links. Counts are not distinct gifts or dollar totals.

## Identity and collision sources

- [The Parker Foundation](https://parker.org/about/foundation) — Identifies Sean Parker as president of the Parker Foundation, PICI founder/chairman, and former Facebook president. Observed 2026-09-20.
- [Parker Institute: Sean Parker biography, April 2018](https://www.parkerici.org/wp-content/uploads/2017/05/sean-parker-bio-042418.pdf) — Identifies Sean Parker as chairman and founder of the Parker Institute for Cancer Immunotherapy. Observed 2026-09-20.

## Scope and unresolved work

Only the exact records and hashes listed below are covered. Known name variants and initials were retrieved across both installed cycles without a giving threshold. Unlisted surname typos, changed surnames and unitemized giving are not covered. Acceptance never changes reported employer or gift accounting. Initial decisions were prepared by Codex using explicitly documented person-specific criteria and then expanded to evidence groups; they are not human sign-offs.
